Game-day write-up: 2026-11-02 (US Eastern)

Injury report 2026-11-02 03:00:00 ET · odds snapshot 20261102T080318Z (Sportsbet, TAB, Dabble, Betr, Neds, Ladbrokes, PointsBet; US books for reference).

Not yet submitted: BKN, BOS, GSW, IND, LAC, LAL, ORL, TOR. Re-run closer to tip.

How to read this: Model fair = the projection alone: minutes as a scenario mix, per-minute rates lined up with tonight's lineup (absences in and out), opponent pace and defence (points, assists), opponent rebounding, and potentials / rebound chances. EV = best AU price × model probability − 1; value = EV ≥ 10% on a rung priced $3–$10. No backtest adjustments. SGM fair prices are model-only: compare with the builder.

My tips

SGMs from one book's own ladder where the builder estimate is at least 1.3× model fair, then singles at 1.2× fair or better (points first). Ranked by margin, weighted to your angles (minutes unlocked, starting centre out, Questionable teammate, potentials). Players who are Questionable themselves are left out. Small = model minutes more than 1.25× his season average. Take each only at the 'take at' price or better.
